# Skills · O · part 20

771 entries, okf-explore to okr-to-jira. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **okf-explore**: Navigate, query, and edit existing Open Knowledge Format (OKF) bundles — directories of markdown files with YAML frontmatter used as agent-readable knowledge bases. Use this skill whenever the user points at an OKF bundle (or a folder of frontmattered markdown that looks like one) and wants to answer questions from it, find a concept, trace relationships between tables/metrics/runbooks/services, summarize what a bundle contains, or update/add concepts to it. Trigger on mentions of OKF, knowledge bundles, LLM wikis, or "what does our knowledge base say about X". ([shreytam/okf-toolkit](https://github.com/shreytam/okf-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/okf-explore))
- **okf-extract**: Guide for extracting content from external sources (websites, manuals, source repos) into OKF-compliant crawl files and topic documents. Covers the full extraction pipeline: identifying source URLs, fetching content (clone repo or HTTP fetch), parsing into structured form, creating crawl files with OKF frontmatter + metadata headers, mapping crawl files to topic docs via provenance tracking, and creating source-map.md. Load when extracting documentation from websites, cloning source repos for content, creating crawl files under docs/&lt;topic&gt;/.crawl/, or building source-map.md provenance indexe… ([rybskiworks/workestrate](https://github.com/rybskiworks/workestrate/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/okf-extract) · ★3)
- **okf-finalize**: Branch-end sweep: reconcile every concept the branch's diff touches, run okfit validate and fix what it reports, check CLAUDE.md pointer coverage, and report what changed. Use at the end of a branch of work that touched the okf/ bundle or its context files, before handing off to a human for commit and changeset. Trigger phrases -- "finalize the bundle", "wrap up the okf changes", "reconcile the docs before merging", "sweep the bundle". ([spencerbeggs/okfit](https://github.com/spencerbeggs/okfit/tree/HEAD/plugin/builds/claude/skills/okf-finalize) · ★1 · 3 copies)
